| 511 | `teacherScript` | Hello, everyone! Welcome to Echo Valley! Look at the mountains. Now listen. Hello! Hello... hello... hello! That's an echo. Today we compare two things. Echo Valley is bigger than the plains. Say it with me. Echo Valley is bigger than the plains. Great! Big, bigger. Tall, taller. When we compare two things, we add e, r and say than. 2つを くらべる ときは ～er than だよ。 The mountains are taller than the trees. The river is longer than the road. Let's practice. An elephant is bigger than a dog. A giraffe is taller than a horse. Now you. Look at your pencil and your eraser. My pencil is longer than my eraser. Wonderful! Now, phonics. Taller. Tallest. Longer. Longest. Just add e, r or e, s, t. Tallest means number one. Okay, let's shout together. Hello, Echo Valley! | Hello, everyone! Welcome to Echo Valley! Look at the mountains. Now listen. Hello! | 512 | `teacherScript` | Hi, everyone! We're in the forest of Echo Valley. Look at these trees. This tree is very tall. That tree is not so tall. So, this tree is taller than that tree. Say it with me. This tree is taller than that tree. Good! And that tree is shorter than this tree. Tall, taller. Short, shorter. たかいは taller、ひくいは shorter だよ。 Let's practice. Stand up, please. Stand on your toes. You are taller now! Crouch down. Now you are shorter. Which tree is taller? This one is. Which tree is shorter? That one is. Now, phonics. Larger. Nicest. Wider. Safest. Large ends with e, so we just add r. Nice ends with e, so we just add s, t. Now look out the window. Find a tall thing. Is it taller than your house? Tell me! | Hi, everyone! We're in the forest of Echo Valley. Look at these trees. This tree is very tall. That tree is not so tall. So, this tree is taller than that tree. Say it with me. This tree is taller than that tree. Good! And that tree is shorter than this tree. Tall, taller. Short, shorter. たかいは taller、ひくいは shorter だよ。 Let's practice. Everyone, stand up! Stand on your toes. You are taller now! Crouch down. Now you are shorter. Which tree is taller? This one is. Which tree is shorter? That one is. Now, phonics. Larger. Nicest. Wider. Safest. Large ends with e, so we just add r. | 516 | `teacherScript` | Hi, everyone! Wow, look at this flower field! There are so many flowers. This flower is pretty. And this flower is very, very pretty. This flower is more beautiful. Say it with me. This flower is more beautiful. Lovely! Beautiful is a long word. We don't say beautifuler. We say more beautiful. ながい ことばは more を つけるよ。 More beautiful. More exciting. More interesting. Let's practice. Which is more exciting, a game or a book? For me, a game is more exciting. Which is more interesting, a movie or a comic? A comic is more interesting! Which flower is more beautiful? I think this one is. Now, phonics. Long words have one strong part. EL-e-phant. kan-ga-ROO. go-RIL-la. OC-to-pus. Say the strong part big and long. Now, look around you. Which is more beautiful, the sky or the sea? Tell me what you think! | Hi, everyone! Wow, look at this flower field! There are so many flowers. This flower is pretty. And this flower is very, very pretty. This flower is more beautiful. Say it with me. This flower is more beautiful. Great! Beautiful is a long word. We don't say beautifuler. We say more beautiful. ながい ことばは more を つけるよ。 More beautiful. More exciting. More interesting. Let's practice. Which is more exciting, a game or a book? For me, a game is more exciting. Which is more interesting, a movie or a comic? A comic is more interesting! Which flower is more beautiful? I think this one is. Now, phonics. Long words have one strong part. EL-e-phant. kan-ga-ROO. go-RIL-la. OC-to-pus. Say the strong part big and long. Now, look around you. Which is more beautiful, the sky or the sea? Tell me what you think! | A+C | British-leaning praise -> American (same rule as W01-16) |

| 518 | `teacherScript` | Hi, everyone! We're in the valley orchard. Look at all the fruit trees! There are apples, oranges and peaches. Mmm. Which do I like? I like apples better than oranges. | 532 | `teacherScript` | Hello, everyone! Today we're visiting the old buildings in the valley. Here is a stone bridge and an old tower. They're both very old. But which is older? The tower door is locked. Let's unlock it with this old key! Now ask your friend a question. Which is bigger, a cat or a dog? | Hello, everyone! Today we're visiting the old buildings in the valley. Which is bigger, the sun or the moon? The sun is. Which is longer, a train or a bus? A train is. Now, phonics. Unlock. Untie. Unpack. Unzip. Lock, unlock. Tie, untie. Un can mean opening or undoing.
